公开交付：下载代码、表格及取证摘录 ZIP · GitHub 源文件

取证文件为移除运行信息后的公开摘录；代码保持原样。评分业务缺口与本版在线验证状态未改变。

GARVEE 页面类型 JSON-LD · 复查版

26 页完整代码；26 页均有渲染证据。新增 3,745 条精确字段路径的来源审查、93 项字段解释、70 条逐页补数要求。类别/活动的 288 个卡片价格已逐商品 URL 与渲染组件重核。

共享实体：不要求每页完整 OnlineStore/WebSite。首页保留完整站点定义，介绍/联系页可完整说明组织；其他页根据发布方、服务、支付、会员或退货政策角色关联稳定 ID。

面包屑：类目样本无组件、无 BreadcrumbList，未借用商品路径；商品改用可见导航，不选隐藏响应式版本。

验证状态按 SHA256 区分版本。本版匹配在线回执 0/54（27版本×2工具）。旧版 11 页 Schema 通过和 27 份 Google 结果均保留，不能沿用为新代码通过。Schema 原任务仍受人工验证码阻塞；Google 当前浏览器工具被 Trusted Types 阻止读取，尚未提交本版代码。评分 4.77/200/166/分布4.425 的业务冲突没有冒充解决。

单表验收清单（确认留空） · 逐字段数值、含义与补数规格 · Schema/Google 支持与全量属性 · 逐字段 CSV · 业务补数 CSV · 字段字典 · 官方指南适用规则 · 实施说明

这是静态真实页面示例及接入规格。未经后台数据一致性和生产复测，不称已上线验收通过；本次未部署到 GARVEE 官网。资料已公开发布到 GitHub。

本页缺什么、字段含义、需要提供什么（1 项）
字段含义当前证据所需真实值来源必要性
OnlineStore.legalName / address / hoursAvailable组织披露、地址及服务角色法定名/总部/客服来源为官方披露；没有独立注册簿验证；无障碍页回拨写 EST，与一般客服 PST 是不同上下文如需法律身份独立验证，提供注册资料；其他地址提供所属实体/分支/用途。各联系渠道分别给星期、时间、时区，不合并成店铺营业。公司主数据＋客服/法务按法律验证/分支扩展目的条件适用

Order / ParcelDelivery具体订单和包裹事实公共页只有查询表单，没有订单/包裹事实仅授权订单上下文用真实状态与物流数据；不向公共页面暴露订单、地址或跟踪号。受保护订单 API实际私有订单详情上下文
ParcelDelivery / Order本页条件字段／数据问题公共查询页没有具体订单或包裹事实。仅在受保护的真实订单上下文中生成；公共页不得放客户订单、地址或跟踪编号。对应商品/内容/政策数据负责人条件适用

本页缺什么、字段含义、需要提供什么（2 项）
字段含义当前证据所需真实值来源必要性
MemberProgramTier.hasTierRequirement等级门槛的积分/周期口径Bronze 注册；Silver 400、Gold 1200、Diamond 2400 pts；FAQ 已说明积分自获取日起滚动有效 1 年，但等级累计/保级周期未核确认积分获取/消费、累计周期、保级/降级/到期条件；等级积分率已按 1/2/3/5 points per USD spent 表达。会员主数据/规则负责人条件适用
MemberProgramTier → Offer.validForMemberTier等级权益与实际商品报价关系可见等级权益文字；未获逐商品优惠价及资格映射每个等级稳定 ID、可用商品/报价范围、货币、价目、折扣叠加与有效时间；不能把计划权益当所有商品统一报价。会员系统＋价格引擎条件适用

字段含义当前证据所需真实值来源必要性
WebPage.url / canonical / support catalog支持站首页身份及帮助内容使用确认的最终 URL https://support.garvee.com/；源 HTML 无 canonical开发若声明 canonical，应由页面实际输出正确标签；提供真实手册/产品映射，不能把生成的 URL 当已存在 canonical。帮助中心 CMSSEO 实现项；不是 JSON-LD 字段造值

      "@type": "WebPageElement",
      "@id": "https://www.garvee.com/collections/rugs#faq",
      "name": "Frequently asked questions",
      "hasPart": [
        {
          "@type": "Question",
          "name": "What rug size should I use in a living room?",
          "acceptedAnswer": {
            "@type": "Answer",
            "text": "Measure the seating group, not just the room. Common layouts place all legs or only the front legs on the rug; leave a deliberate, even border where possible."
          }
        },
        {
          "@type": "Question",
          "name": "How much larger should a dining rug be than the table?",
          "acceptedAnswer": {
            "@type": "Answer",
            "text": "It should extend far enough for chair legs to remain on the rug when chairs are pulled out. Measure your chairs in use because table shape and chair depth vary."
          }
        },
        {
          "@type": "Question",
          "name": "Does a non-slip backing replace a rug pad?",
          "acceptedAnswer": {
            "@type": "Answer",
            "text": "Not always. Floor finish, rug size, traffic, backing condition, and manufacturer instructions determine whether an additional compatible pad is appropriate."
          }

本页缺什么、字段含义、需要提供什么（7 项）
字段含义当前证据所需真实值来源必要性
Product.offers.seller该报价实际卖方先前统一按 GARVEE 推定；条款有平台第三方表述；新代码已省略每个商品真实合同卖方的名称、稳定 ID、URL；确认品牌、平台运营者和销售主体的关系。商品商家主数据＋法务/交易负责人条件适用
Product.name（Google 历史非严重长度建议）商品真实名称与展示长度建议历史Google商家信息曾提示部分真实长名称字符串长度问题；本版尚未在线复测如要消除建议，PIM须提供真实简洁商品名并同步可见页面；不在Schema中单独删词/裁剪来冒充官方名称，也不把非严重建议说成词汇不合法。PIM/商品内容负责人Google历史建议；待本版复测核实
ShippingService / OfferShippingDetails.shippingRate / deliveryTime配送费用、地区、处理/运输时效FAQ 2–4、Express 3–5、Truck 7–10/10–15 工作日口径冲突；处理时间另为 1–2 工作日统一处理与运输时间、服务级别、工作日、PST 截单时间、邮编/州限制、费用与门槛；不能把 1–2+3–5 写成运输本身或无条件全美免费。物流/运费引擎＋政策负责人条件适用
BreadcrumbList类目本页的实际导航链当前代码没有 BreadcrumbList；原始和渲染 DOM 都没有类目面包屑组件若后续增加导航，提供本类目真实展示层级、名称和目标 URL；不能取 PDP 的路径作为类目路径。导航/类目主数据实际组件存在时
ItemList.numberOfItems / inventory total已加载条目数与全库数量区别快照分别 84/204 个去重已加载商品，不是全目录总数生产中取本次 SSR/分页实际输出数组长度；全类目商品总量若需要另取同筛选条件的后端总量，不能混用。商品列表 API生产实时绑定
Offer.seller本页条件字段／数据问题站点品牌身份不等于每个商品的合同卖方；服务条款声称平台作为第三方。逐商品提供真实 seller 商家名称、身份 ID 与 URL；未确认前省略 seller，不统一写 GARVEE。对应商品/内容/政策数据负责人条件适用
Product card availability / sku / GTIN / aggregateRating本页条件字段／数据问题列表只使用卡片可核实的名称、图片、链接及当前美元价格；不从商品名称推 SKU/GTIN，不继承有冲突的评分。生产接入实际商品源后补全真实适用字段；列表语义有效不等于商品详情富结果资格。对应商品/内容/政策数据负责人条件适用

本页缺什么、字段含义、需要提供什么（23 项）
字段含义当前证据所需真实值来源必要性
Product.aggregateRating.ratingValue平均分可见 4.77；星级比例计算 4.425，不一致同商品/商品组、同审核/时间口径的真实均分；与前端显示和星级分布对齐。Number，量表 1–5。评价平台/API 及评价负责人恢复完整多评论 Google 版本前必须解决
Product.aggregateRating.ratingCount评分总条数；不自动为去重人数未建立可信总评分数量；200 Reviews 不足以证明此字段非负整数；明确是否包含仅打星未写评论、删除/审核/导入评分和统计范围；不得以 reviewCount 代填。评价平台/API条件适用
Product.aggregateRating.reviewCount评论总条数可见 200 Reviews；原 JSON-LD 166；5 条只是抓取样本与页面、均分和分布同口径的整数；解释 200 与 166 的区别，不自行判成分别对应评分/评论。评价平台/API与 ratingCount 至少一个可信正计数满足 Google；两者均有数据则分别提供
AggregateRating.bestRating / worstRating / aggregation scope评分量表及汇总范围界面 1–5 星；商品、商品组、变体及时间范围未确认bestRating=5、worstRating=1 仅在真实量表仍为 1–5 时；补 scopeType、scopeId、关联 SKU、审核/导入规则和数据版本。评价平台＋商品主数据条件适用
非 Schema 的 uniqueReviewerCount去重评价人数尚无证据；不是 ratingCount 的同义词仅业务确实需要人数时提供去重用户口径；不要自造 AggregateRating.uniqueReviewerCount 属性。评价平台/数据团队业务可选指标，非标准属性
ProductGroup.productGroupID / hasVariant / variesBy真实变体分组和所属关系可见颜色/尺寸选项，但缺完整变体主数据真实父组 ID、每个变体独立 SKU/GTIN/URL、颜色尺寸、价格库存；确认多 URL 还是单 URL 变体策略。PIM / Shopify 商品与变体 API条件适用
Product.gtin / mpn / itemCondition商品标识及新旧状况SKU PHO_16KHFWWY 真实公开；GTIN/MPN 未获；NewCondition 仅旧元数据支持，已省略如确实分配 GTIN，提供合法码与位数；MPN 由制造商提供。新/二手/翻新状况须主数据和正文一致；没有编码则不造。PIM/制造商有实际编码或状况数据且适用时
Product.offers.seller该报价实际卖方先前统一按 GARVEE 推定；条款有平台第三方表述；新代码已省略每个商品真实合同卖方的名称、稳定 ID、URL；确认品牌、平台运营者和销售主体的关系。商品商家主数据＋法务/交易负责人条件适用
Product.name（Google 历史非严重长度建议）商品真实名称与展示长度建议历史Google商家信息曾提示部分真实长名称字符串长度问题；本版尚未在线复测如要消除建议，PIM须提供真实简洁商品名并同步可见页面；不在Schema中单独删词/裁剪来冒充官方名称，也不把非严重建议说成词汇不合法。PIM/商品内容负责人Google历史建议；待本版复测核实
Offer.priceSpecification / validForMemberTier会员优惠价与资格可见普通价 127.99 USD、会员价 115.20；等级条件未核公开普通价继续为 127.99；另提供会员价、USD、真实等级 @id、资格和叠加/有效期规则，不能假定属于 Bronze。价格引擎＋会员系统条件适用
Offer.priceSpecification.membershipPointsEarned购买这笔报价可得积分可见 128 积分/$1.28，但和等级、实际支付金额的关系未核真实积分整数、适用等级、计算基数/舍入/兑换率；确认 128 是否按该报价计算。会员系统＋价格引擎条件适用
Product.additionalProperty[Weave Type] / weight scope编织工艺与重量范围渲染 DOM 两张表分别有 Tufted/Flat Woven；保留真实 32.4 lb，未猜编织值确认当前 SKU/变体实际编织工艺；确认 32.4 是产品净重还是包装/运输重量，并同步冲突表。PIM/制造商条件适用
Product.review[].itemReviewed逐条评论所属商品/变体5 条真实可见评论，但样例对整个产品页归属，变体统计范围未核提供评论所属 productId/variantId、作者公开显示名、正文、真实日期、独立星级及 moderation 状态。评价平台条件适用
QAPage / Question用户问答内容Q&A=0；未生成虚构问答有真实问答后提供问题、作者/日期、答案、可提交答案机制；按实际单问题页/模块选择模型。问答系统有真实内容后条件适用
ShippingService / OfferShippingDetails.shippingRate / deliveryTime配送费用、地区、处理/运输时效FAQ 2–4、Express 3–5、Truck 7–10/10–15 工作日口径冲突；处理时间另为 1–2 工作日统一处理与运输时间、服务级别、工作日、PST 截单时间、邮编/州限制、费用与门槛；不能把 1–2+3–5 写成运输本身或无条件全美免费。物流/运费引擎＋政策负责人条件适用
MerchantReturnPolicy / Offer.hasMerchantReturnPolicy组织级退货规则与商品例外组织级 merchantReturnLink 是 Google Option B；正文已披露30天及 Return Address、按原因邮费规则，不应称地址/天数全部缺失链接方案可保持；若展开 Option A 或商品例外，提供国家、窗口起点/天数、方法、原因对应费用/币种、restocking 和实际退货地址。总部不可代填。售后政策＋商品例外规则条件适用，非链接版必填缺陷
Offer.seller / itemCondition本页条件字段／数据问题seller 曾按站点品牌推定；NewCondition 仅来自原 JSON-LD，渲染正文未明确新旧状况。提供该 SKU 实际卖方及新/二手/翻新状态；已省略无法由当前可见信息证明的两个字段。对应商品/内容/政策数据负责人条件适用
Offer.priceSpecification / validForMemberTier / membershipPointsEarned本页条件字段／数据问题渲染可见会员价 $115.20、购买可得 128 积分；尚未核实对应会员等级、资格及叠加条件。提供同一 SKU 的普通价、会员价、USD、适用等级稳定 ID、积分整数及资格规则；会员价不能替代公开价，不能猜测 Bronze 等级。对应商品/内容/政策数据负责人条件适用
AggregateRating本页条件字段／数据问题源 JSON-LD reviewCount=166；渲染页和组件=200；星级分布折算均值约 4.425，也不等于显示的 4.77。统一真实评价主数据及统计对象，确认商品/变体归属后输出汇总；当前不生成 AggregateRating。单条评论按组件所示保留，归属仍需业务验收。对应商品/内容/政策数据负责人条件适用
ProductGroup / variesBy / hasVariant本页条件字段／数据问题页面可选择颜色和尺寸，但当前采样未核实稳定商品组 ID、全部变体与对应报价。变体模型列入完整适用清单；接入真实组/变体映射后生成，不能凭相似标题或 SKU 推组关系。对应商品/内容/政策数据负责人条件适用
gtin / mpn / priceValidUntil / shippingDetails本页条件字段／数据问题没有核实全球贸易编号、制造商料号、价格到期日期或完整配送模型。SKU 不冒充 GTIN/MPN；价格期限不猜测，运输依赖真实运费/区域/时效。对应商品/内容/政策数据负责人条件适用
Weave Type本页条件字段／数据问题两张规格表分别为 Tufted 和 Flat Woven。业务统一规格后同步正文与 additionalProperty；当前略去冲突字段。对应商品/内容/政策数据负责人条件适用
QAPage / Question / Answer本页条件字段／数据问题商品问答组件实际为 Questions and Answers (0)。不得生成空问题或假答案；多问题商品页也不能为了 Google 套单问题 QAPage。对应商品/内容/政策数据负责人条件适用

Review / AggregateRating / ItemList本页条件字段／数据问题独立页面在原始 HTML 和两次渲染取证中只有 Product Reviews 标题，没有评论条目。此页示例只能输出有依据的页面及面包屑；恢复真实评论及所评商品关联后再输出 Review。商品样本另含真实评论示例。对应商品/内容/政策数据负责人条件适用

ShippingService / OfferShippingDetails.shippingRate / deliveryTime配送费用、地区、处理/运输时效FAQ 2–4、Express 3–5、Truck 7–10/10–15 工作日口径冲突；处理时间另为 1–2 工作日统一处理与运输时间、服务级别、工作日、PST 截单时间、邮编/州限制、费用与门槛；不能把 1–2+3–5 写成运输本身或无条件全美免费。物流/运费引擎＋政策负责人条件适用

FAQ 业务事实一致性本页条件字段／数据问题FAQ 写运输 2–4 工作日，与配送页 Express 3–5 及 Truck 7–10/10–15 口径不同。示例忠实保留该页问答；上线前统一业务范围与条款，再同步正文和 JSON-LD。Google FAQ 富结果已移除。对应商品/内容/政策数据负责人条件适用

字段含义当前证据所需真实值来源必要性
BlogPosting.author / author.url各文章卡片的真实作者36/12 个已加载卡片；未逐篇取得真实作者信息按文章 ID/URL 返回真实作者及介绍页，与详情 CMS 同源；不统一拿站点名填作者。CMS/内容 API条件适用

In this article, we will briefly help you understand the types of rugs available, the important features they come with, and then we will shed light on the most crucial aspect – rug size and rug placement, and more. This video is sourced from YouTube@Dear Modern Table of Contents Types of Rugs Based on Use, Shape, and Style Why Do Rug Size and Placement Matter? Recommended Rug Sizes for Different Rooms Choosing Different Rug Shapes Rug Placement Tips Measuring and Selecting the Right Rug Size Additional Tips Types of Rugs Based on Use, Shape, and Style Rugs are functional textile pieces that provide comfort, and elevate the look of your space. They can be categorized based on use, shape, and style. By Use: These include area rugs that are used particularly in bedrooms and living rooms. The standard sizes available for these are 9x12 area rugs. Then, you have bathroom rugs that are used in bathrooms and come with anti-slip and water-absorbing features. The kitchen rug comes with stain-resistant and anti-fatigue features. Outdoor rugs are designed for withstanding harsh weather conditions and come with waterproof, fade-resistant features, etc. By Shape: You have rectangular rugs that are most popularly used, round area rugs that are used to highlight certain furniture pieces, runner rugs that are narrow and long, etc. By Style: Rugs are available in various styles. You have modern rugs that come with sleek and minimal designs, boho rugs that come with interesting patterns and prints, vintage rugs that have intricate designs, transitional rugs that are a blend of both modern and traditional designs, etc. Here are a few rugs on GARVEE that you must take a look at. • Check out the GARVEE 9x12 Area Rug Modern Moroccan Washable Rug • Check out the GARVEE Runner Rugs for Hallway 2x6 Kitchen Rugs Why Do Rug Size and Placement Matter? The two main reasons as to why rug size and placement matter are: Visual Impact The correct rug size and rug placement play a crucial role in creating a balanced look for your space. Rugs help elevate a piece of furniture and tie together various elements of your home décor. Whether you choose standard 9x12 area rugs or 8x10 rugs, oversized rugs, small rugs, runner rugs, boho rugs , vintage rugs, modern rugs, wool rugs, jute rugs, shag rugs, etc., it’s important that you choose one that is suitable – in terms of size, shape, design – for your space so that it creates visual interest and enhances the aesthetics of your home. Functionality Rugs aren’t just to beautify your floor, they are absolutely functional and help in various ways, hence, it’s crucial to choose the right size and place them correctly. A few ways in which they are functional include: • Floor Protection: Rugs help keep your floor safe from scratches, stains, scuffs, and wear on your wooden and tile floors. • Noise Reduction: Rugs help absorb sound, thereby, helping create a quieter space for you. • Extra Cushioning: Rugs add an additional layer of cushioning which provides comfort and warmth. • Safety Purpose: Anti-slip features keep rugs in place securely, thereby, preventing slipping which otherwise could happen if there wasn’t any rug on the floor. Recommended Rug Sizes for Different Rooms Below are some recommended rug sizes for different rooms. Living Room The living room is that section of your home where you would place area rugs. Recommended sizes for living rooms are the standard 9x12 area rugs, 8x10 rugs, 6x9 rugs and 5x7 rugs. This also solely depends on the size of your living room as well as the number of furniture pieces you have. If you choose a large area rug such as a 9x12 or an 8x10 area rug, then place the furniture entirely on the rug for a unified look. If you have smaller rugs such as a 5x7 rug, then ensure the furniture is partially placed on the rug for a balanced appearance. Bedroom Rugs in bedrooms add style, warmth, and comfort. You can use a 5x8 rug at the foot of your bed or you can go for larger area rugs that can be placed beneath the bed. Make sure to place the rug in the center if you are placing it under the bed. Also, ensure that the rug is visible equally on all sides for a cohesive look. Dining Room For your dining space, the rug you choose should provide coverage and comfort. You will need a rug large enough to cover the table and all the chairs even when you pull them out. This would mean that a 6x9 rug, 8x10 rug or 9x12 area rug would be ideal. Also, ensure that there is at least 24-30 inches of rug space left beyond the tables and chairs. Hallway Runner rugs are perfect for hallways. The standard recommended size would be 2x6 or 2x8 inch rugs. For a symmetrical look, leave at least 4-6 inches of exposed floor on each side. Small Seating Area For small seating areas, round rugs, 5x7 rugs, or 3x5 rugs would be ideal. These rugs can be placed under a coffee table with two chairs on either side. Choosing Different Rug Shapes Let’s take a look at the different rug shapes that are used in households. Rectangular Rugs: Rectangular rugs are very versatile and are most commonly used in homes. These rugs are ideal for various room layouts and furniture arrangements. Besides, they complement both traditional and modern interior designs. Round Rugs: Round rugs are the most ideal for small spaces and under round furniture pieces such as round dining tables, coffee tables, etc. They also make for the perfect nursery rugs as they add a touch of warmth and coziness to any space. Unusual-Shaped Rugs: Unusual-shaped rugs are perfect for creating those unique focal points and adding some character to your home decoration. Here are a few rugs on GARVEE that you must check out: • Check out the GARVEE 3ft Boho Round Geometric Rug • Check out the GARVEE Large Area Rug 8x10 Vintage Rug Rug Placement Tips When your rug is placed correctly, it brings about a balanced look, enhances the functionality of the room, makes the room look very organized, and adds to the aesthetics of your home. • To achieve this balance, you first have to make sure the rug complements the room’s proportion. The rug should ideally be big or small, depending on the size of the room, and furniture to avoid an overcrowded or undersized look. • When placing a rug in the living room, make sure to place the rug fully under the furniture or partially to give some definition to your seating section. Now this will also depend on the size of the rug that you have chosen. If you have chosen a standard 9x12 area rug or 8x10 rug, then you might be able to place the furniture fully, but if you have a 6x9 rug or a 5x7 rug, then most likely you will be able to place the furniture partially. • Rugs in bedrooms can either be placed completely under the bed or at the foot of the bed. If the rug is placed under the bed, then make sure that the rug is extended 18-24 inches beyond the bed on all sides (foot and the sides) for a balanced look. You can also just place a soft and comfortable runner rug at the foot of the bed if you do not want it under. You can also place round area rugs, however, for a more balanced look, consider placing it just under the foot of the bed instead of placing it completely. This way you have a little of the rug under the bed and the rest extending outside, creating a stylish and cozy look and feel. • For your dining space, the rug should cover the all furniture. This means it should be placed under the table as well as under chairs, even when pulled out. Doing so not only provides stability but also protects the floors when pulling the chairs in and out. Measuring and Selecting the Right Rug Size Here are some tips to help you measure and select the right rug size. • Visualize how you would want your rug to be placed on the floor and then using a measuring tape outline your desired rug dimensions. • Measure your space accurately by taking into account both the size of your space and the layout of your furniture. • The rug size that you choose should be proportionate to your space for an organized look. Additional Tips Some additional tips. • When selecting rugs for your rooms ensure that the color and material you choose match or complement the style and décor of your space. • For enhanced safety and extension of your rug’s life, use rug pads. • You can layer smaller rugs over large ones to add some depth and texture. • For the busy areas of your home, use darker-colored rugs and low-pile rugs. • You can experiment with unusual shapes for an added touch of style and uniqueness. • Rotate rugs periodically to prevent uneven wear or fading. • Choose thicker material rugs for larger rooms as they can reduce echo or noise. • Vacuum and spot-clean rugs regularly to keep them clean. For ensuring a perfectly functional and stylish space, the right rug size and rug placement are essential. Doing so helps you achieve a cohesive and unified look. Whether you choose a standard 9x12 area rug, a smaller 5x7 rug, a boho rug, a round rug, etc., it’s important to choose rugs that suit your space and complement your home décor. 本页缺什么、字段含义、需要提供什么（8 项）
字段含义当前证据所需真实值来源必要性
Product.offers.seller该报价实际卖方先前统一按 GARVEE 推定；条款有平台第三方表述；新代码已省略每个商品真实合同卖方的名称、稳定 ID、URL；确认品牌、平台运营者和销售主体的关系。商品商家主数据＋法务/交易负责人条件适用
Product.name（Google 历史非严重长度建议）商品真实名称与展示长度建议历史Google商家信息曾提示部分真实长名称字符串长度问题；本版尚未在线复测如要消除建议，PIM须提供真实简洁商品名并同步可见页面；不在Schema中单独删词/裁剪来冒充官方名称，也不把非严重建议说成词汇不合法。PIM/商品内容负责人Google历史建议；待本版复测核实
ShippingService / OfferShippingDetails.shippingRate / deliveryTime配送费用、地区、处理/运输时效FAQ 2–4、Express 3–5、Truck 7–10/10–15 工作日口径冲突；处理时间另为 1–2 工作日统一处理与运输时间、服务级别、工作日、PST 截单时间、邮编/州限制、费用与门槛；不能把 1–2+3–5 写成运输本身或无条件全美免费。物流/运费引擎＋政策负责人条件适用
Offer.priceValidUntil / Event促销报价失效时间与事件资格有真实价格/活动区块；未获真实结束时间，促销本身不是 Event若价格有真实截止时刻才提供日期/时间及时区；Event 仅实际事件时另建。页面标题 2026 不证明全年有效。营销排期＋价格系统真实限时或真实事件存在时
ItemList.numberOfItems / inventory total已加载条目数与全库数量区别快照分别 84/204 个去重已加载商品，不是全目录总数生产中取本次 SSR/分页实际输出数组长度；全类目商品总量若需要另取同筛选条件的后端总量，不能混用。商品列表 API生产实时绑定
Offer.seller本页条件字段／数据问题站点品牌身份不等于每个商品的合同卖方；服务条款声称平台作为第三方。逐商品提供真实 seller 商家名称、身份 ID 与 URL；未确认前省略 seller，不统一写 GARVEE。对应商品/内容/政策数据负责人条件适用
Product card availability / sku / GTIN / aggregateRating本页条件字段／数据问题列表只使用卡片可核实的名称、图片、链接及当前美元价格；不从商品名称推 SKU/GTIN，不继承有冲突的评分。生产接入实际商品源后补全真实适用字段；列表语义有效不等于商品详情富结果资格。对应商品/内容/政策数据负责人条件适用
Offer.validFrom / validThrough / discount conditions本页条件字段／数据问题样本标题提及 Save $45，但没有核实完整适用条件与起止日期。不得把普通促销建为 Event；优惠条件与时间取活动主数据，不按抓取日或标题年份推断。对应商品/内容/政策数据负责人条件适用
